这个是G2的GDK 和示例代码，你先研究一下
我先按“文档总览 → Python示例能力 → 运动/导航/感知接口 → 后续可改造方向”来梳理。

Thought for 21s

我已经先研究了一遍。这个资料主要分成 G2/G02 的 GDK开发文档 和一组 Python示例代
码。
核心结论如下：
1. GDK定位
GDK 是给 Genie/G2 机器人做二次开发的 SDK，主要覆盖：运动控制、传感器读取、
SLAM/导航、任务调度、C++/Python/ROS2 接入等能力。文档里明确支持 C++、
Python、ROS2，并且常见部署方式是开发机与机器人网线直连，机器人调试 IP 为
10.42.1.101 ，开发机配置到 10.42.1.xxx 网段。
agibot_docs

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

1/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

开发的基本流程
标准流程是：

2. Python

import agibot_gdk
agibot_gdk.gdk_init()
Python
robot
= agibot_gdk.Robot()

Run

然后通过 Robot() 、 Camera() 、 Pnc() 、 Slam() 、 Imu() 、 Lidar() 等对象分
别访问机器人本体、相机、导航、SLAM、IMU、雷达等模块。Python示例里也基本都
遵循这个结构。
3. 运动控制部分
你上传的 mc_example.py 是基础版关节控制工具，支持键盘切换关节、调整关节角
度、播放 saved_commands 里的 JSON动作序列。它使用 JointControlReq() ，设置
life_time 、 joint_names 、 joint_positions 、 joint_velocities ，然后调用
robot.joint_control_request() 。
g2_motion_control.py 是更完整的版本，已经加入了 22个关节名称、关节限位、安
全检查、动作录制、动作编排和回放逻辑，后续如果要做“跳舞动作”“武术动作”“固定
动作编排”，这个文件最有价值。
4. 机器人状态与夹爪控制
robot_demo.py 里覆盖了关节状态、全身状态、末端执行器状态、运动控制状态、电
源状态、头部控制、夹爪控制等测试项。它可以作为排查机器人是否正常连接、是否急
停、是否有错误码的基础诊断工具。
文档里也有 move_ee_pos() 的夹爪/末端执行器控制示例，不同末端类型如
omnipicker 、 dahuan 、 ctek90d 、 o10_t2 、 o12_t2 对应的位置范围和关节数量
不同，这部分后面做抓取时必须核对实际末端型号。
5. 相机与视觉部分
camera_demo.py 支持头部双目左/右、左手相机、右手相机、头部深度相机、头部彩
色相机等；核心接口是 get_latest_image(camera_type, timeout) 和
get_nearest_image() ，代码里也处理了 JPEG、PNG、RGB、BGR、GRAY8、
GRAY16、RS2_FORMAT_Z16 等图像格式。
camera_web_viewer.py 是 Flask Web相机查看器，适合先用浏览器确认哪些相机有
画面、画面延迟和可用性。
6. 导航与SLAM
pnc_example.py 是最小导航示例：创建 agibot_gdk.Pnc() ，构造 NaviReq() ，设
置目标点 position 和 orientation，然后调用 pnc.normal_navi(target) 。

G2 GDK研究总结
pnc_example

用于 SLAM 状态、里程计、定位/建图相关信息读取，后面如果要让机
器人自己去某个点、记地图、导航到指定位置，会重点用这部分。
7. 综合任务：取瓶子
g2_fetch_bottle.py 是最复杂的综合示例，目标流程是：导航到A点 → 相机识别瓶
子 → 深度估计3D位置 → 手臂接近 → 夹爪抓取 → 导航到B点 → 放置。它把
Pnc.normal_navi / relative_move 、 Camera.get_latest_image + OpenCV 、
Robot.end_effector_pose_control 、 Robot.move_ee_pos 集成到了一个状态机
里。
这个文件更像“应用层任务模板”，后续如果你想做“识别某个物体并抓起来/递送/放到
指定位置”，应该以它为基础改。
我已经把这套资料的结构和关键接口理清了。后面你可以直接问我：
“帮我改成控制G2跳舞”、“帮我写一个抓取流程”、“帮我解释某个API”、“帮我检查代码为
什么跑不起来”，我就可以基于这些文件继续往下做。

我想用pi0.5在智元上做VLA，我该怎么做根据上面的GDK
示例代码，你写的详细一点
我会把它拆成可落地的工程方案：先确认 pi0.5 的输入/输出形态，再映射到 GDK 里的相
机、状态、底盘、机械臂、夹爪接口，最后给你一套代码架构和实施步骤。
Thought for 1m 7s

可以做，但不要一上来就让 pi0.5 直接闭环控制整台智元 G2。正确路线是：先用 GDK 把
G2 封装成一个“机器人环境”，再让 pi0.5 只负责输出动作块，最后通过一个 Action
Adapter 动作适配层 把 pi0.5 的动作安全地转换成 GDK 的机械臂、夹爪、底盘控制命令。
下面按工程落地来讲。

动作块

控制接口
智元 执行

本质上是 VLA，也就是输入视觉、语言和机器人本体状态，输出机器人动作。论文里
说，VLA 的输入通常包括一张或多张图像、机器人 proprioceptive state，也就是关节位置
等本体状态，以及自然语言任务指令；输出是 action 或 action chunk。
Physical Intelligence 的 pi0.5 运行时还会先生成一个高层子任务文本，再生成低层机器人
电机命令；官方介绍里提到低层命令是 50步、1秒长度的连续动作块。
对应到 G2，GDK 这边刚好能提供你需要的东西：相机、关节状态、SLAM导航、底盘移
动、机械臂末端控制、夹爪控制。GDK文档本身也明确说它适合机器人运动控制、传感器
数据获取、建图、移动以及和大模型对接。

G2 GDK研究总结

官方要求推理至少需要 8GB 以上显存，LoRA 微调需要约 22.5GB 以上显存，全量
微调需要 70GB 以上显存；它也推荐 Ubuntu 22.04。
所以你的开发机最好是 Ubuntu 22.04 + NVIDIA GPU，至少 RTX 4090 级别会舒服很多。
OpenPI

GitHub

3.

先不要直接用 pi05_base 控 G2

现在有 pi05_base 、 pi05_droid 、 pi05_libero 等 checkpoint。官方说明
pi05_base 是用于微调的基础模型， pi05_droid 是在 DROID 数据上微调的模型，能用
于推理或继续微调。
但是 G2 不是 DROID，也不是 ALOHA。不同机器人之间至少有这些差异：
OpenPI

GitHub

1.
2.
3.
4.
5.
6.
7.

相机位置不同
关节数量不同
末端执行器不同
动作空间不同
坐标系不同
控制频率不同
机械臂运动范围不同

所以你有两条路线：
路线A：先做“VLA + GDK技能库”
pi0.5 负责理解视觉和语言，输出高层动作或短动作；
GDK 负责执行导航、抓取、夹爪、回收等安全技能。
路线B：真端到端 VLA
采集 G2 自己的数据，把 pi05_base / pi05_droid 微调到 G2 的 action space。

我建议你先走 路线A，能最快看到效果；后面再走 路线B，做真正的 G2 VLA。

import time
import cv2
import numpy as np
import agibot_gdk

class G2ObservationAdapter:
def __init__(self):
self.robot = agibot_gdk.Robot()
self.camera = agibot_gdk.Camera()
time.sleep(2)
self.head_camera = agibot_gdk.CameraType.kHeadColor
self.left_wrist_camera = agibot_gdk.CameraType.kHandLeftColor
self.right_wrist_camera = agibot_gdk.CameraType.kHandRightColor
def decode_image(self, image):
if image is None or image.data is None:
return None
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.JPEG:
arr = np.frombuffer(image.data, np.uint8)
img = cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
return cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.PNG:
arr = np.frombuffer(image.data, np.uint8)
img = cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
return cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.UNCOMPRESSED:
arr = np.frombuffer(image.data, dtype=np.uint8)

return arr.reshape((image.height, image.width, 3))
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.BGR:
img = arr.reshape((image.height, image.width, 3))
return cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
raise RuntimeError(f"Unsupported image format: {image.encoding}")
def get_rgb(self, camera_type):
image = self.camera.get_latest_image(camera_type, 1000.0)
rgb = self.decode_image(image)
if rgb is None:
raise RuntimeError("Failed to get camera image")
return cv2.resize(rgb, (224, 224))
def get_joint_vector(self):
joint_states = self.robot.get_joint_states()
states = joint_states["states"]
names = []
positions = []
velocities = []
for s in states:
names.append(s["name"])
positions.append(float(s["position"]))
velocities.append(float(s["velocity"]))
return {
"joint_names": names,
"joint_positions": np.array(positions, dtype=np.float32),
"joint_velocities": np.array(velocities, dtype=np.float32),
}
def get_observation(self, prompt: str):
head = self.get_rgb(self.head_camera)
left_wrist = self.get_rgb(self.left_wrist_camera)
state = self.get_joint_vector()
return {
"observation/exterior_image_1_left": head,
"observation/wrist_image_left": left_wrist,
"observation/state": state["joint_positions"],
"prompt": prompt,

6.

第二阶段：启动 pi0.5 Policy Server

不要把 GDK 和 OpenPI 强行塞进一个 Python 环境。GDK 示例默认 Python 3.10，
OpenPI/JAX/PyTorch 依赖比较重，容易冲突。OpenPI 官方也支持 remote inference，也
就是模型运行在另一个 server 里，通过 websocket 把动作流回机器人端，这正适合 G2 +
外部 GPU 工作站的场景。
OpenPI 侧大概这样：
GitHub

git clone --recurse-submodules git@github.com:Physical-Intelligence/openpi
cd openpi
GIT_LFS_SKIP_SMUDGE=1 uv sync
Bash
GIT_LFS_SKIP_SMUDGE=1
uv pip install -e .

然后先用官方 checkpoint 测试推理：
from openpi.training import config as _config
from openpi.policies import policy_config
from openpi.shared import download
config = _config.get_config("pi05_droid")
checkpoint_dir = download.maybe_download(
"gs://openpi-assets/checkpoints/pi05_droid"
)
policy = policy_config.create_trained_policy(config, checkpoint_dir)
example = {
"observation/exterior_image_1_left": ...,
"observation/wrist_image_left": ...,
"prompt": "pick up the bottle",
}

7.

第三阶段：做 G2 Action Adapter

这里是最关键的地方。
pi0.5 输出的是动作块，但这个动作块不一定天然适配 G2。你需要定义 G2 的 action
space。我建议先用 末端笛卡尔增量动作，不要一开始用 22个关节角。
建议动作格式：
单臂动作：
[

dx, dy, dz,
#
droll, dpitch, dyaw, #
gripper
#
]

末端位置增量，单位 m
末端姿态增量，单位 rad
夹爪开合，0~1

也就是每一步 7维。pi0.5 一次输出 50步，就是：
action_chunk.shape = [50, 7]

GDK

这边用：

Robot.end_effector_pose_control()
Robot.move_ee_pos()

控制机械臂末端位姿
控制夹爪

注意：GDK 文档明确说 end_effector_pose_control() 要求发送端以 50Hz 频率发布控
制命令，不允许阶跃信号，而且接口无碰撞检测，所以必须自己做限幅、插值、安全检查。
agibot_docs

里也有 get_motion_control_status() 可以检查运动模式、错误码、
frame_names，并且有 move_ee_pos() 控制左右夹爪的示例。
动作适配器大概这样：
robot_demo.py

import time
import math
import numpy as np
import agibot_gdk

LEFT_GROUP = 4
RIGHT_GROUP = 8

def clamp(x, lo, hi):
return max(lo, min(hi, x))

def quat_from_rpy(roll, pitch, yaw):
cr = math.cos(roll / 2)
sr = math.sin(roll / 2)
cp = math.cos(pitch / 2)
sp = math.sin(pitch / 2)
cy = math.cos(yaw / 2)
sy = math.sin(yaw / 2)
return np.array([
sr * cp * cy - cr * sp * sy,
cr * sp * cy + sr * cp * sy,
cr * cp * sy - sr * sp * cy,
cr * cp * cy + sr * sp * sy,
], dtype=np.float32)

class G2ActionAdapter:
def __init__(self, robot, arm="left"):
self.robot = robot
self.arm = arm
self.rate_hz = 50.0
self.dt = 1.0 / self.rate_hz
self.life_time = 0.02

1.5cm
self.max_step_rot = 0.05
#
0.05rad
self.workspace_min = np.array([0.20, -0.70, 0.30])
self.workspace_max = np.array([0.80, 0.70, 1.30])
def get_current_ee_pose(self):
status = self.robot.get_motion_control_status()

def send_ee_pose(self, pos, quat):
req = agibot_gdk.EndEffectorPose()
req.life_time = self.life_time
req.group = LEFT_GROUP if self.arm == "left" else RIGHT_GROUP
if self.arm == "left":
p = req.left_end_effector_pose
else:
p = req.right_end_effector_pose
p.position.x = float(pos[0])
p.position.y = float(pos[1])
p.position.z = float(pos[2])
p.orientation.x = float(quat[0])
p.orientation.y = float(quat[1])
p.orientation.z = float(quat[2])
p.orientation.w = float(quat[3])
return self.robot.end_effector_pose_control(req)
def send_gripper(self, value_0_to_1):
value = clamp(float(value_0_to_1), 0.0, 1.0)

import time
import agibot_gdk
from g2_observation import G2ObservationAdapter
from g2_action_adapter import G2ActionAdapter
from pi05_client import Pi05Client

class G2VLARuntime:
def __init__(self, prompt, arm="left"):
self.prompt = prompt
if agibot_gdk.gdk_init() != agibot_gdk.GDKRes.kSuccess:
raise RuntimeError("GDK init failed")
self.obs_adapter = G2ObservationAdapter()
self.robot = self.obs_adapter.robot
self.action_adapter = G2ActionAdapter(self.robot, arm=arm)
self.policy = Pi05Client(host="127.0.0.1", port=8000)
def safety_check(self):
status = self.robot.get_whole_body_status()
if status["left_arm_error"] != 0:
raise RuntimeError(f"Left arm error: {status['left_arm_error']
if status["right_arm_error"] != 0:
raise RuntimeError(f"Right arm error: {status['right_arm_error
if status["chassis_error"] != 0:
raise RuntimeError(f"Chassis error: {status['chassis_error']}"
def run(self):
print(f"VLA task prompt: {self.prompt}")

5. pi0.5

导航用 GDK 的 Pnc.normal_navi() 。你上传的 pnc_example.py 里就是创建

底盘速度控制可以参考 move_chassis.py ，它通过

pnc.move_chassis(twist) 发送底盘速度。
所以移动操作建议是：
pnc_example

move_chassis

actions.jsonl

每一帧记录：
{
"timestamp": 123456789,
"prompt": "pick up the red bottle",
"joint_names": ["idx01_body_joint1", "..."],
"joint_positions": [0.1, 0.2, "..."],
"ee_position": [0.45, 0.22, 0.88],
"ee_quaternion": [0, 0, 0, 1],
"action": [0.002, -0.001, 0.000, 0.0]
} JSON

动作最好记录成你后面要执行的同一种 action space。例如你准备用 dx, dy, dz,
gripper 控制，那数据里也记录这个，而不是直接记录遥控按键。
OpenPI 官方微调流程要求先把自己的数据转换成 LeRobot dataset，然后定义数据处理和
训练 config，再计算 normalization statistics，最后运行训练。官方 README 里也明确把
微调拆成三步：转换到 LeRobot dataset、定义训练配置、启动 policy server 做推理。

- ping 10.42.1.101

第2步：跑通 observation
- 能拿到 head image
- 能拿到 wrist image
- 能拿到 joint_states
- 能保存一帧 observation 到本地
第3步：跑通 pi0.5 离线推理
- 用 dummy observation
- 打印 action_chunk.shape
- 不接机器人
第4步：接 mock action
- 不用 pi0.5
- 固定输出小的 dx, dy, dz
- 验证 G2 机械臂能按 50Hz 小幅移动
第5步：接真实 pi0.5
- 只执行前 5~10 个 action
- 每步限幅
- 随时准备急停

- 用遥操作或脚本示教采集 50~200 条单任务 demonstration
- 转 LeRobot dataset
- 微调 pi05_base 或 pi05_droid
第7步：闭环部署
- OpenPI server 推理
- G2 runtime 执行
- 加安全边界、超时、失败恢复
13.

最重要的安全限制

这几个必须加：

限幅

1. action
dx/dy/dz

每步
先限制到 0.5~1.5cm。
2. 工作空间限制

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

19/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

不要让末端离身体太近、太低、太高、太靠外。
3. 夹爪限幅
先标定真实夹爪的 open/close 范围。
4. 只执行短chunk
pi0.5 一次可能输出 50步，第一版只执行前 5~10步。
5. 50Hz平滑控制
end_effector_pose_control 不能阶跃，必须连续小步发送。
6. 碰撞风险自担
GDK 文档明确说该接口无碰撞检测。
7. 人手必须靠近急停
尤其第一次真机闭环时。

14.

一句话总结方案

你要做的不是“直接把 pi0.5 放到智元 G2 上跑”，而是：
用 GDK 把 G2 封装成一个机器人环境；
用 camera_demo / robot_demo 采集 observation；
用 OpenPI pi0.5 输出 action_chunk；
用 Action Adapter 把 action_chunk 转成 GDK 的 end_effector_pose_control
最后采集 G2 自己的数据，把 pi0.5 微调到 G2 的相机、末端和动作空间。

最推荐你先改 g2_fetch_bottle.py ，它已经是“导航 + 视觉 + 抓取 + 放置”的应用骨架，
只需要把里面原来的颜色检测 / YOLO 检测模块替换成 pi0.5 policy.infer() ，再把输出
动作接到 ArmController.servo_move() 和 move_ee_pos() 。

Thought for 1m 44s

我看完你上传的 pi0.5 论文、说明和 openpi 源码后，结论很明确：
你这台智元 G2 要实现 pi0.5 的“泛化”，不能只把 pi05_droid 跑起来就结束。真正要做的
是：把 G2 变成 openpi 能理解的一种新机器人平台，然后采集 G2 自己的数据，用 pi0.5
的训练范式去微调。
也就是：

1.

身

先纠正一个关键认知：pi0.5 的泛化来自“数据配方”，不是模型名字本

论文里讲得很清楚，它的泛化不是简单靠一个大模型，而是靠混合数据源：多机器人
数据、高层语义子任务预测、网页视觉语义数据、人工语言指导、低层动作数据一起训练。
论文里提到，pi0.5 不只是学机械臂动作，还会先预测类似 “pick up the plate” 这样的高层
子任务，再根据子任务生成低层动作块。
所以你在 G2 上做泛化，也要复刻这个思想：
pi0.5

pi05

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

2026/4/27 17:35

不要只采：
“拿瓶子 → 放瓶子” 的低层轨迹。
还要采：
1. 不同物体
2. 不同桌面
3. 不同光照
4. 不同摆放
5. 不同语言说法
6. 每一步对应的高层子任务标签
7. 失败/恢复/重试场景

10.42.1.101 ，开发机配置为 10.42.1.102 或同网段地址。GDK 提供运动控制、传感器

这一阶段你可以先用 pi05_droid ，但不要直接把它的输出完整执行到 G2。因为 DROID

你要采集：

你上传的 g2_fetch_bottle.py 已经很接近一个完整 VLA 任务骨架：它把 SLAM 导航、相
机视觉、末端位姿伺服、夹爪控制放在一个状态机里，流程是“导航到 A 点 → 检测瓶子 →
抓取 → 导航到 B 点 → 放置”。它还明确用了 Camera.get_latest_image 、
Pnc.normal_navi / relative_move 、 Robot.end_effector_pose_control 和
Robot.move_ee_pos 。
所以你应该把它改成 G2 数据采集器 + VLA runtime，而不是只当成抓瓶子 demo。

4. G2

的 action space 应该怎么定义

这是成败关键。
你有三种选择：

方案 1：关节空间控制
action = [
left_joint_1_delta,
left_joint_2_delta,
...
left_joint_7_delta,

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

25/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结
gripper

]

优点：直接对应 joint_control_request 。
缺点：泛化差，模型要自己学运动学，数据需求更大。
适合：跳舞、固定动作、关节轨迹模仿。

方案 2：末端空间控制，推荐第一版用这个
action = [
dx, dy, dz,
droll, dpitch, dyaw,
gripper
]

优点：更接近“抓取/放置”的语义，数据效率更高。
缺点：需要你用 GDK 的末端伺服控制去执行。
你上传的 g2_fetch_bottle.py 已经明确用到了 Robot.end_effector_pose_control ，
并且注释里写了这个控制需要 50Hz 伺服执行。
所以第一版最推荐：
g2_fetch_bottle

G2-pi0.5-v1 action_dim = 7
0: dx
1: dy
2: dz
3: droll
4: dpitch
5: dyaw
6: gripper

训练时 action 是连续值；执行时把它变成 GDK 的末端目标位姿和夹爪位置。

方案 3：移动操作全身 action

如果你要对齐 pi0.5 论文里的 mobile manipulator，最终可以做：
https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

import dataclasses
import numpy as np

import einops
from openpi import transforms
from openpi.models import model as _model

def _parse_image(image):
image = np.asarray(image)
if np.issubdtype(image.dtype, np.floating):
image = (255 * image).astype(np.uint8)
if image.shape[0] == 3:
image = einops.rearrange(image, "c h w -> h w c")
return image

@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)
class G2Inputs(transforms.DataTransformFn):
model_type: _model.ModelType
def __call__(self, data: dict) -> dict:
head_image = _parse_image(data["observation/head_image"])
left_wrist = _parse_image(data["observation/left_wrist_image"])
state = np.concatenate([
np.asarray(data["observation/left_arm_joint_position"]),
np.asarray(data["observation/left_gripper_position"]),
]).astype(np.float32)
inputs = {
"state": state,
"image": {
"base_0_rgb": head_image,
"left_wrist_0_rgb": left_wrist,
"right_wrist_0_rgb": np.zeros_like(head_image),
},
"image_mask": {
"base_0_rgb": np.True_,
"left_wrist_0_rgb": np.True_,
"right_wrist_0_rgb": np.False_,
},
}
if "actions" in data:
inputs["actions"] = np.asarray(data["actions"]).astype(np.floa
if "prompt" in data:
inputs["prompt"] = data["prompt"]
return inputs

inputs=[
_transforms.RepackTransform(
{
"observation/head_image": "head_image",
"observation/left_wrist_image": "left_wrist_image"
"observation/left_arm_joint_position": "left_arm_j
"observation/left_gripper_position": "left_gripper_
"actions": "actions",
"prompt": "prompt",
}
)
]
)

inputs=[g2_policy.G2Inputs(model_type=model_config.model_type)
outputs=[g2_policy.G2Outputs(action_dim=7)],
)
model_transforms = ModelTransformFactory()(model_config)
return dataclasses.replace(
self.create_base_config(assets_dirs, model_config),
repack_transforms=repack_transform,
data_transforms=data_transforms,
model_transforms=model_transforms,

响表现。
pi05

7.

采多少数据才有意义

你不要按“一个任务采 10 条”这种思路做。对泛化来说，数量和多样性都重要。

最小验证版

任务数：1 个
例子：抓杯子放托盘
物体：5 种杯子
位置：每种 10 个位置
光照：2 种
背景：2 种
每种组合：1~2 条
总量：200~400 条 episode

这个能验证 G2 + pi0.5 微调链路。
https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

32/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

初级泛化版
任务数：3~5 个
例如：
- 拿杯子放托盘
- 拿瓶子放盒子
- 把毛巾放进篮子
- 关抽屉
- 推动物体到指定区域
物体：20~50 个
场景：5~10 个桌面 / 房间角落
每个任务：300~800 条
总量：1500~4000 条 episode

这个才开始有“换物体、换桌面、换光照”的泛化。

接近 pi0.5 思路的版本
低层动作数据：
- 单臂桌面操作
- 双臂协作
- 简单移动操作
高层子任务数据：

- clean table
- pick up cup
- move to tray
- open drawer
- place item into drawer

语言指导数据：
- 人在旁边一步步说下一步做什么
- 只记录 observation + instruction + selected subtask
视觉语义数据：
- 图像问答
- 物体框标注
- 场景描述

论文里 pi0.5 用了约 400 小时真实移动操作数据，同时还用了大量其他机器人、网页和语义
数据；所以你小规模数据可以做“局部泛化”，但不要期待立刻达到论文里“新房间打扫厨房/
卧室”的程度。
pi05

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

数据采集方式：优先改 g2_fetch_bottle.py

你现在最好的入口不是从零写，而是改这个：
g2_fetch_bottle.py

它已经有：
INIT
NAVI_TO_A
WAIT_NAVI_A
DETECT_BOTTLE
APPROACH_BOTTLE
GRASP_BOTTLE
LIFT_BOTTLE
NAVI_TO_B
WAIT_NAVI_B
PLACE_BOTTLE
RELEASE_BOTTLE
RETRACT_ARM
DONE

你可以把每个状态都当成一个 subtask label：
状态机状态

subtask

标签

NAVI_TO_A

navigate to the pickup area

DETECT_BOTTLE

find the bottle

APPROACH_BOTTLE

move gripper above the bottle

GRASP_BOTTLE

close gripper on the bottle

LIFT_BOTTLE

lift the bottle

PLACE_BOTTLE

move bottle above the target area

RELEASE_BOTTLE

release the bottle

RETRACT_ARM

retract the arm

这样你不只是采低层动作，还采到了 pi0.5 论文里很重视的 高层语义子任务数据。

11. G2

执行 action 的方式

如果你的 action 是：

[dx, dy, dz, droll, dpitch, dyaw, gripper]

执行器要做：
当前末端位姿
+
action 增量

↓
Robot.end_effector_pose_control()
+
Robot.move_ee_pos()

一定要加限幅：
dx = clip(dx, -0.01, 0.01)
dy = clip(dy, -0.01, 0.01)
dz = clip(dz, -0.01, 0.01)
droll = clip(droll, -0.03, 0.03)
dpitch = clip(dpitch, -0.03, 0.03)
dyaw
= clip(dyaw, -0.03, 0.03)
Python= clip(gripper, 0.0, 0.6)
gripper

Run

第一版甚至可以只开 4 维：
[dx, dy, dz, gripper]

姿态先固定不动。这样风险最低。

15.

最推荐的第一版目标

你现在不要直接做“G2 打扫房间”。第一版目标应该是：
单臂桌面 VLA：
自然语言指令：
“把桌上的某个物体放到托盘里”
泛化要求：
1. 物体换 10 种
2. 位置随机
3. 桌面背景随机
4. prompt 说法随机
5. 能自己完成抓取和放置
G2

这条路线最现实，也最接近 OpenPI 官方 DROID/LIBERO 示例的适配难度。
16.

最核心的一句话

上做 pi0.5 泛化，本质不是“调用 pi0.5”，而是做一个 G2 版本的 pi0.5 数据闭环：GDK
采集多模态 observation，定义 G2 action space，转换 LeRobot 数据，写
G2Inputs/G2Outputs 和 TrainConfig，用 pi05_base/pi05_droid 微调，最后通过
websocket policy server 回到 G2 执行。
G2

本机 / GDK环境：

负责运行 pi0.5 模型推理、训练、微调。
两边通过 websocket / policy server 通信。
G2

官方也明确支持远程推理：模型可以跑在另一台服务器上，通过 websocket 把
action 流式传给机器人端，这正适合你的 G2。

合。GDK 文档里也提到 PTP 用在多传感器数据融合、分布式系统同步、实时控制和数据采

import numpy as np
import agibot_gdk

def ensure_dir(path):
os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)

def image_data_to_bytes(data):
if hasattr(data, "tobytes"):
return data.tobytes()
return bytes(data)

def decode_image(image):
"""

camera_web_viewer.py
JPEG / PNG / UNCOMPRESSED RGB / BGR / GRAY /
"""
if image is None:
return None

Pasted text

先解释一下 nano 崩溃
你前面 nano 崩了：

nano: /home/agi/app/lib/libncursesw.so.6: no version information available
Bash Nano crashed! Code: 11.
Sorry!

这是因为你 source ~/app/env.sh 后， LD_LIBRARY_PATH 指向了
/home/agi/app/lib ，导致 nano 加载了 G2 app 自带的 libncursesw.so.6 ，和系统
nano 不兼容。
以后记住：
写代码/编辑文件：不要 source ~/app/env.sh
运行 GDK 程序：才 source ~/app/env.sh

如果已经 source 了，又想用 nano，可以这样临时清掉库路径：
env Bash
-u LD_LIBRARY_PATH nano g2_runtime/xxx.py

更稳的方式是以后直接用 cat > 文件 <<'PY' 写脚本，不用 nano。

下一步：做“连续采集 10 秒 observation”

def image_data_to_bytes(data):
if hasattr(data, "tobytes"):
return data.tobytes()
return bytes(data)

def decode_image(image):
if image is None:
return None
if not hasattr(image, "data") or image.data is None or len(image.data)
return None
raw = image_data_to_bytes(image.data)
try:
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.JPEG:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, np.uint8)
return cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.PNG:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, np.uint8)
return cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.UNCOMPRESSED:
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.RGB:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
rgb = arr.reshape((image.height, image.width, 3))
return cv2.cvtColor(rgb, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.BGR:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
return arr.reshape((image.height, image.width, 3))
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.GRAY8:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
gray = arr.reshape((image.height, image.width))
return cv2.cvtColor(gray, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.GRAY16:

scale = target_w / float(w)

def add_label(img, text):
out = img.copy()
cv2.rectangle(out, (0, 0), (out.shape[1], 32), (0, 0, 0), -1)
cv2.putText(
out,
text,
(8, 23),
cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
0.6,
(255, 255, 255),
2,
cv2.LINE_AA,
)
return out

def pad_to_height(img, target_h):
h, w = img.shape[:2]
if h >= target_h:
return img
pad = target_h - h
return cv2.copyMakeBorder(
img,
0,
pad,
0,
0,
cv2.BORDER_CONSTANT,
value=(20, 20, 20),
)

def make_row(frame):
frame_id = frame["frame_id"]
cameras = frame.get("cameras", [])
imgs = []
for cam in cameras:
name = cam.get("name")
path = cam.get("path")
img = cv2.imread(path)
if img is None:
img = np.zeros((240, 320, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
cv2.putText(img, f"missing: {name}", (10, 120),

imgs.append(img)
if not imgs:
return None
max_h = max(i.shape[0] for i in imgs)
imgs = [pad_to_height(i, max_h) for i in imgs]
return np.hstack(imgs)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import os
import json
import time

G2 GDK研究总结

def ensure_dir(path):
os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)

def image_data_to_bytes(data):
if hasattr(data, "tobytes"):
return data.tobytes()
return bytes(data)

def decode_image(image):
if image is None or not hasattr(image, "data") or image.data is None:
return None
raw = image_data_to_bytes(image.data)
try:
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.JPEG:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, np.uint8)
return cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.PNG:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, np.uint8)
return cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.UNCOMPRESSED:
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.RGB:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
rgb = arr.reshape((image.height, image.width, 3))
return cv2.cvtColor(rgb, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.BGR:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
return arr.reshape((image.height, image.width, 3))
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.GRAY8:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
gray = arr.reshape((image.height, image.width))
return cv2.cvtColor(gray, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)
return None
except Exception as e:
print(f"[WARN]
return None

图像解码失败: {e}")

def serialize_joint_states(joint_states):
states = []

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

for s in joint_states.get("states", []):
states.append({
"name": s.get("name"),
"position": float(s.get("position", 0.0)),
"velocity": float(s.get("velocity", 0.0)),
"effort": float(s.get("effort", 0.0)),
"motor_position": float(s.get("motor_position", 0.0)),
"motor_current": float(s.get("motor_current", 0.0)),
"error_code": int(s.get("error_code", 0)),
})
return {
"nums": joint_states.get("nums"),
"timestamp": joint_states.get("timestamp"),
"states": states,
}

def save_camera_frame(camera, camera_type, name, frame_id, image_dir):
image = camera.get_latest_image(camera_type, 1000.0)
if image is None:
return None
bgr = decode_image(image)
if bgr is None:
return None
filename = f"{name}_{frame_id:06d}.jpg"
out_path = os.path.join(image_dir, filename)
cv2.imwrite(out_path, bgr)
return {
"name": name,
"path": out_path,
"timestamp_ns": int(image.timestamp_ns),
"width": int(image.width),
"height": int(image.height),
"encoding": str(image.encoding),
"color_format": str(image.color_format),
}

prompt = "do nothing"
subtask = "keep still"
duration_s = 5.0
fps = 2.0

def send_left_gripper(robot, gripper_pos):
"""

只控制左夹爪，不控制机械臂。
gripper_pos 先用保守范围。

"""

"left_ee_state": {
"joint_position": float(gripper_pos)
},
"right_ee_state": {
"joint_position": 0.0
}
}
ret = robot.move_ee_pos(action)
return ret

Thought for 17s

很好，这个报错不是你操作错了，是我前面按 robot_demo.py 里的旧写法用了 dict ，但
你当前这台 G2 的实际 move_ee_pos() 绑定接口要求的是 agibot_gdk.JointStates 对
象，不是 Python 字典。
你的报错关键行是：
TypeError: move_ee_pos(): incompatible function arguments.
supported:
(self: Robot, arg0: agibot_gdk.agibot_gdk.JointStates) -> int
Invoked with: robot, {'left_ee_state': {'joint_position': 0.0}, ...}

所以原因很明确：传参类型错了。
GDK 文档里也写了， move_ee_pos() 的参数是 JointStates 对象，需要设置 group 、
target_type 、 states 、 nums ；其中 group 应该是 "left_tool" 、 "right_tool"
或 "dual_tool" 。
官方示例里控制左夹爪就是这样写的：创建 agibot_gdk.JointStates() ，设置
group="left_tool" 、 target_type="omnipicker" ，再创建
agibot_gdk.JointState() ，设置 position ，最后调用
robot.move_ee_pos(joint_states_left) 。

import time
import agibot_gdk

def move_left_gripper(robot, pos, target_type="omnipicker"):
joint_states = agibot_gdk.JointStates()
joint_states.group = "left_tool"
joint_states.target_type = target_type
joint_state = agibot_gdk.JointState()
joint_state.position = float(pos)
joint_states.states = [joint_state]
joint_states.nums = len(joint_states.states)
return robot.move_ee_pos(joint_states)

def main():

joint_state.position = float(gripper_pos)

Thought for 16s

很好，这次报错已经把真实夹爪范围暴露出来了。现在不是 JointStates 类型问题了，类
型已经对了；新的问题是 夹爪位置方向写反了。
日志里关键提示是：
end_effector joint[0] position out of range [-0.785, 0]: 0.05

也就是说，你这台 G2 左夹爪当前实际接受的范围是：
最小值：-0.785
最大值：0

所以 0.05 、 0.1 、 0.15 都是非法的，因为它们大于 0。 0.0 能成功，是因为它在范围
内。你的测试里 0.0 ret: 0 成功，到了 0.05 就报超限。
虽然文档里 omnipicker 示例写的是 [0, 1] ，但文档也说明“关节位置值超出对应末端类
型取值范围会返回 InvalidInput / 抛异常”，而你当前机器运行时配置实际范围是 [-0.785,
0] ，所以应以真机报错为准。

gripper_episode_*")

def camera_map(cameras):
result = {}
for cam in cameras:
name = cam.get("name")
path = cam.get("path")
if name == "head_color":
result["head"] = path
elif name == "left_wrist_color":
result["left_wrist"] = path
elif name == "right_wrist_color":
result["right_wrist"] = path
return result

def joint_state_to_vectors(joint_states):
states = joint_states.get("states", [])
joint_names = []
joint_positions = []
joint_velocities = []
for s in states:
joint_names.append(s.get("name"))
joint_positions.append(float(s.get("position", 0.0)))
joint_velocities.append(float(s.get("velocity", 0.0)))
return joint_names, joint_positions, joint_velocities

第 2 步：创建 dataset manifest

现在我们要给所有 JSONL 建一个索引文件，后面数据多了好管理。

cat > g2_runtime/g2_build_dataset_manifest.py <<'PY'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import os
import json
import glob
from datetime import datetime

def read_jsonl_stats(path):
frame_count = 0
action_dims = set()
joint_dims = set()
prompts = set()
subtasks = set()
image_keys_set = set()
first = None
last = None
with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
for line in f:
item = json.loads(line)
if first is None:
first = item
last = item
frame_count += 1
action_dims.add(len(item["action"]["values"]))
joint_dims.add(len(item["state"]["joint_positions"]))
prompts.add(item.get("prompt"))

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

160/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结
subtasks.add(item.get("subtask"))
image_keys_set.add(tuple(sorted(item["images"].keys())))
return {
"path": path,
"frame_count": frame_count,
"action_dims": sorted(action_dims),
"joint_dims": sorted(joint_dims),
"prompts": sorted([p for p in prompts if p is not None]),
"subtasks": sorted([s for s in subtasks if s is not None]),
"image_keys": [list(x) for x in sorted(image_keys_set)],
"first_frame_id": first["frame_id"] if first else None,
"last_frame_id": last["frame_id"] if last else None,
}

def main():
root = os.path.expanduser("~/g2_openpi")
jsonl_dir = os.path.join(root, "training_jsonl")
out_path = os.path.join(root, "dataset_manifest.json")
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(jsonl_dir, "*.jsonl")))
manifest = {
"dataset_name": "g2_vla_raw",
"version": "0.1",
"created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
"jsonl_count": len(files),
"episodes": [],
}
total_frames = 0
for path in files:
stats = read_jsonl_stats(path)
manifest["episodes"].append(stats)
total_frames += stats["frame_count"]
manifest["total_frames"] = total_frames
with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
json.dump(manifest, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
print("=== G2 Dataset Manifest ===")
print("jsonl_count:", manifest["jsonl_count"])
print("total_frames:", manifest["total_frames"])
print("output:", out_path)
for ep in manifest["episodes"]:

if __name__ == "__main__":
main()

注意一个小问题

你现在多次 source ~/app/env.sh 后， LD_LIBRARY_PATH 和 PYTHONPATH 里重复了很
多次路径。这个暂时不影响运行，但后面建议：
一个终端里 source 一次就够了。
不要每执行一个命令都 source 一遍。

# G2 VLA Dataset Spec v0.1
## 1. Dataset Goal
This dataset is used for Agibot G2 VLA / pi0.5 adaptation experiments.
Current stage:
- Build a stable G2 data collection pipeline.
- Collect synchronized images, robot state, gripper feedback, and action c
- Convert raw episodes into training JSONL.
- Prepare for future LeRobot / OpenPI conversion.
This version is not intended for real manipulation training yet.
It is a pipeline validation dataset.

--## 3. Raw Episode Structure
Raw data is saved under:
g2_data/
gripper_episode_xxxxx/
images/
frames/
meta.json
prompt.txt
Each frame JSON contains:
- frame_id
- time_unix
- prompt
- subtask
- joint_states
- end_state
- cameras
- action
--## 4. Training JSONL Structure
Converted data is saved under:
training_jsonl/
gripper_episode_xxxxx.jsonl
Each line is one frame sample.
Main fields:
- dataset_version
- robot
- episode_id
- frame_id

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import os
import json
import glob
import random
from datetime import datetime

def main():
root = os.path.expanduser("~/g2_openpi")
jsonl_dir = os.path.join(root, "training_jsonl")
out_path = os.path.join(root, "dataset_splits.json")
files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(jsonl_dir, "*.jsonl")))
if len(files) < 2:
raise RuntimeError("

至少需要 2 条 JSONL 才能划分 train / val")

random.seed(42)
shuffled = files[:]
random.shuffle(shuffled)
val_count = max(1, int(round(len(shuffled) * 0.2)))
val_files = sorted(shuffled[:val_count])
train_files = sorted(shuffled[val_count:])

"total_jsonl": len(files),

}

print("val:", len(val_files))

print("\nTrain files:")
for p in train_files:
print(" -", os.path.basename(p))
print("\nVal files:")
for p in val_files:
print(" -", os.path.basename(p))

if __name__ == "__main__":
main()

import json
import cv2
from pathlib import Path

def load_json(path):
with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
return json.load(f)

def read_jsonl(path):
with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
for line in f:
if line.strip():
yield json.loads(line)

def check_image(path):
if not os.path.exists(path):
raise RuntimeError(f"image path not found: {path}")
img = cv2.imread(path)
if img is None:
raise RuntimeError(f"image unreadable: {path}")
h, w = img.shape[:2]
return {
"width": int(w),
"height": int(h),
"channels": int(img.shape[2]) if len(img.shape) == 3 else 1,
}

def build_prompt(item):
prompt = item.get("prompt", "")
subtask = item.get("subtask", "")
return f"Task: {prompt}. Current subtask: {subtask}."

def convert_item(item, split_name):
images = item["images"]
head_path = images["head"]
left_wrist_path = images["left_wrist"]
right_wrist_path = images["right_wrist"]
image_info = {
"base_0_rgb": check_image(head_path),
"left_wrist_0_rgb": check_image(left_wrist_path),

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

"right_wrist_0_rgb": check_image(right_wrist_path),
}
joint_positions = item["state"]["joint_positions"]
left_gripper_position = item["state"]["left_gripper_position"]
if left_gripper_position is None:
left_gripper_position = 0.0
state = list(joint_positions) + [float(left_gripper_position)]
action = item["action"]["values"]
if len(joint_positions) != 22:
raise RuntimeError(f"joint_positions dim != 22: {len(joint_positio
if len(state) != 23:
raise RuntimeError(f"state dim != 23: {len(state)}")
if len(action) != 7:
raise RuntimeError(f"action dim != 7: {len(action)}")
return {
"dataset_version": "g2_openpi_intermediate_v0.1",
"robot": "agibot_g2",
"split": split_name,
"episode_id": item["episode_id"],
"frame_id": item["frame_id"],

},

"22_joint_positions",
"left_gripper_feedback_position"
],
},

},

def convert_split(split_name, files, out_path):
count = 0
with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
for jsonl_path in files:
for item in read_jsonl(jsonl_path):
converted = convert_item(item, split_name)
out.write(json.dumps(converted, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
count += 1
return count

def main():
root = Path(os.path.expanduser("~/g2_openpi"))
split_path = root / "dataset_splits.json"
out_dir = root / "openpi_intermediate"
out_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
splits = load_json(split_path)
train_out = out_dir / "train.jsonl"
val_out = out_dir / "val.jsonl"
train_count = convert_split("train", splits["train"], train_out)
val_count = convert_split("val", splits["val"], val_out)
meta = {
"dataset_name": "g2_openpi_intermediate",
"version": "0.1",
"train_path": str(train_out),
"val_path": str(val_out),
"train_frames": train_count,
"val_frames": val_count,
"state_dim": 23,

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

"action_dim": 7,
"image_keys": [
"base_0_rgb",
"left_wrist_0_rgb",
"right_wrist_0_rgb"
],
}
meta_path = out_dir / "meta.json"
with open(meta_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
json.dump(meta, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
print("=== G2 OpenPI Intermediate Export ===")
print("train:", train_out, "frames:", train_count)
print("val:", val_out, "frames:", val_count)
print("meta:", meta_path)
print("
done")

import json

def validate_file(path, expected_split):
errors = []
count = 0
with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
for line_idx, line in enumerate(f):
item = json.loads(line)
count += 1
if item["split"] != expected_split:
errors.append(f"{path} line {line_idx}: wrong split")
if item["state"]["dim"] != 23:
errors.append(f"{path} line {line_idx}: state dim != 23")
if len(item["state"]["values"]) != 23:
errors.append(f"{path} line {line_idx}: state len != 23")
if item["action"]["dim"] != 7:
errors.append(f"{path} line {line_idx}: action dim != 7")
if len(item["action"]["values"]) != 7:
errors.append(f"{path} line {line_idx}: action len != 7")
for key in ["base_0_rgb", "left_wrist_0_rgb", "right_wrist_0_r
img_path = item["image_paths"].get(key)
if not img_path:
errors.append(f"{path} line {line_idx}: missing image
continue
if not os.path.exists(img_path):
errors.append(f"{path} line {line_idx}: image not exis
continue
img = cv2.imread(img_path)
if img is None:
errors.append(f"{path} line {line_idx}: image unreadab
return count, errors

def read_jsonl(path, max_items=6):
items = []
with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
for line in f:
if line.strip():
items.append(json.loads(line))
if len(items) >= max_items:
break
return items

def load_resize(path, size=224):
img = cv2.imread(path)
if img is None:
raise RuntimeError(f"

无法读取图像: {path}")

img = cv2.resize(img, (size, size), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
return img

def add_label(img, text):
out = img.copy()
cv2.rectangle(out, (0, 0), (out.shape[1], 28), (0, 0, 0), -1)
cv2.putText(
out,
text,
(6, 20),
cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
0.45,
(255, 255, 255),
1,
cv2.LINE_AA,
)
return out

def make_row(item):
ep = item["episode_id"]
fid = item["frame_id"]
paths = item["image_paths"]
base = load_resize(paths["base_0_rgb"])
left = load_resize(paths["left_wrist_0_rgb"])
right = load_resize(paths["right_wrist_0_rgb"])
base = add_label(base, f"base | f{fid}")
left = add_label(left, f"left_wrist | f{fid}")

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

198/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结
right = add_label(right, f"right_wrist | f{fid}")
row = np.hstack([base, left, right])
info_bar = np.zeros((36, row.shape[1], 3), dtype=np.uint8)
action = item["action"]["values"]
gripper = action[-1]
text = f"{ep} | frame={fid} | gripper_cmd={gripper}"
cv2.putText(
info_bar,
text,
(8, 24),
cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
0.5,
(255, 255, 255),
1,
cv2.LINE_AA,
)
return np.vstack([info_bar, row])

def main():
root = Path("~/g2_openpi").expanduser()
train_path = root / "openpi_intermediate" / "train.jsonl"
out_dir = root / "openpi_intermediate" / "preview"
out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
items = read_jsonl(train_path, max_items=6)
if not items:
raise RuntimeError("train.jsonl

为空")

rows = [make_row(item) for item in items]
grid = np.vstack(rows)
out_path = out_dir / "openpi_224_preview.jpg"
cv2.imwrite(str(out_path), grid)
print("=== OpenPI 224 Preview ===")
print("samples:", len(items))
print("output:", out_path)
print("
224x224 preview generated")

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import os
import sys
import json
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np

让脚本能

#
import g2_openpi_dataset_loader.py
ROOT = Path("~/g2_openpi").expanduser()
sys.path.append(str(ROOT / "g2_runtime"))
from g2_openpi_dataset_loader import G2OpenPIDataset

def collect_split(split):
ds = G2OpenPIDataset(split=split, image_size=224)
states = []
actions = []
prompts = []
for i in range(len(ds)):
sample = ds[i]
states.append(sample["state"])
actions.append(sample["action"])
prompts.append(sample["prompt"])
states = np.stack(states, axis=0)
actions = np.stack(actions, axis=0)
return {
"split": split,
"count": len(ds),
"states": states,
"actions": actions,
"prompts": prompts,
}

def stats_array(x):
return {
"shape": list(x.shape),
"mean": x.mean(axis=0).astype(float).tolist(),
"std": x.std(axis=0).astype(float).tolist(),
"min": x.min(axis=0).astype(float).tolist(),
"max": x.max(axis=0).astype(float).tolist(),
}

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

def main():
train = collect_split("train")
val = collect_split("val")
all_states = np.concatenate([train["states"], val["states"]], axis=0)
all_actions = np.concatenate([train["actions"], val["actions"]], axis=
out = {
"dataset_name": "g2_openpi_intermediate",
"version": "0.1",
"train_count": train["count"],
"val_count": val["count"],
"total_count": int(all_states.shape[0]),
"state_dim": int(all_states.shape[1]),
"action_dim": int(all_actions.shape[1]),
"state_stats": stats_array(all_states),
"action_stats": stats_array(all_actions),
"left_gripper_feedback_stats": {
"index_in_state": 22,
"mean": float(all_states[:, 22].mean()),
"std": float(all_states[:, 22].std()),
"min": float(all_states[:, 22].min()),
"max": float(all_states[:, 22].max()),
},
"left_gripper_action_stats": {
"index_in_action": 6,
"unique_values": sorted(set(float(x) for x in all_actions[:, 6
"mean": float(all_actions[:, 6].mean()),
"std": float(all_actions[:, 6].std()),
"min": float(all_actions[:, 6].min()),
"max": float(all_actions[:, 6].max()),
},
"prompt_examples": sorted(set(train["prompts"] + val["prompts"])),
}
out_path = ROOT / "openpi_intermediate" / "norm_stats.json"
with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
json.dump(out, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
print("=== G2 OpenPI Norm Stats ===")
print("train_count:", out["train_count"])
print("val_count:", out["val_count"])
print("total_count:", out["total_count"])
print("state_dim:", out["state_dim"])
print("action_dim:", out["action_dim"])
print("left gripper feedback:", out["left_gripper_feedback_stats"])
print("left gripper action:", out["left_gripper_action_stats"])

Pasted text

先看一个重要现象

你的 state_stats 里前 22 个关节的 std 几乎是 0，这不是错误。
原因是：目前这个数据集只控制夹爪，机械臂、身体、底盘都没动。所以 22 个关节位置基
本恒定。真正变化的是：
state[22] = left_gripper_feedback
action[6] = left_gripper_target

所以这个 v0.1 数据集只能验证：
看到图像 + 当前状态 → 输出夹爪目标值

它还不能训练抓取、移动末端、移动机械臂。这个判断很重要，说明你现在的数据是“管线
验证数据集”，不是“泛化抓取数据集”。

import numpy as np
import agibot_gdk
ROOT = Path("~/g2_openpi").expanduser()
sys.path.append(str(ROOT / "g2_runtime"))
from g2_openpi_transforms import G2Outputs
from g2_action_safety_adapter import G2ActionSafetyAdapter

def serialize_joint_states(robot):
joint_states = robot.get_joint_states()
positions = []
for s in joint_states.get("states", []):
positions.append(float(s.get("position", 0.0)))
if len(positions) != 22:
raise RuntimeError(f"joint dim should be 22, got {len(positions)}"
return positions

def read_left_gripper_feedback(robot):
end_state = robot.get_end_state()
left = end_state.get("left_end_state", {})
joints = left.get("end_states", [])
if not joints:
return 0.0, {
"controlled": left.get("controlled"),
"type": left.get("type"),
"name": None,
"position": None,
"err_code": None,
}
j = joints[0]
feedback = {
"controlled": left.get("controlled"),
"type": left.get("type"),
"name": left.get("names", [None])[0],
"position": float(j.get("position", 0.0)),
"velocity": float(j.get("velocity", 0.0)),
"effort": float(j.get("effort", 0.0)),
"err_code": int(j.get("err_code", 0)),
}

def get_realtime_observation(robot, prompt):
joint_positions = serialize_joint_states(robot)

raise RuntimeError(f"state shape should be (23,), got {state.shape
return {
"prompt": prompt,
"state": state,
"left_gripper_feedback": left_gripper_feedback,
"time_unix": time.time(),
}

def move_left_gripper(robot, target_position):
joint_states = agibot_gdk.JointStates()
joint_states.group = "left_tool"
joint_states.target_type = "omnipicker"
joint_state = agibot_gdk.JointState()
joint_state.position = float(target_position)
joint_states.states = [joint_state]
joint_states.nums = len(joint_states.states)
return robot.move_ee_pos(joint_states)

→ GDK 执行左夹爪
→ 读取真实反馈
→ 保存 runtime log

server = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
server.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
server.bind((HOST, PORT))
server.listen(1)
try:
while True:
conn, addr = server.accept()
print("\nclient connected:", addr)
with conn:
buffer = b""
while True:
data = conn.recv(4096)
if not data:
break
buffer += data
while b"\n" in buffer:
line, buffer = buffer.split(b"\n", 1)
if not line.strip():
continue
try:
req = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
resp = handle_request(req)
except Exception as e:
resp = {
"ok": False,
"error": repr(e),
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import sys
import json
import time
import socket
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import numpy as np
import agibot_gdk
ROOT = Path("~/g2_openpi").expanduser()
sys.path.append(str(ROOT / "g2_runtime"))
from g2_openpi_transforms import G2Outputs
from g2_action_safety_adapter import G2ActionSafetyAdapter

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

246/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

SERVER_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
SERVER_PORT = 8765

def serialize_joint_states(robot):
joint_states = robot.get_joint_states()
positions = []
for s in joint_states.get("states", []):
positions.append(float(s.get("position", 0.0)))
if len(positions) != 22:
raise RuntimeError(f"joint dim should be 22, got {len(positions)}"
return positions

def read_left_gripper_feedback(robot):
end_state = robot.get_end_state()
left = end_state.get("left_end_state", {})
joints = left.get("end_states", [])
if not joints:
return 0.0, {
"controlled": left.get("controlled"),
"type": left.get("type"),
"name": None,
"position": None,
"err_code": None,
}
j = joints[0]
feedback = {
"controlled": left.get("controlled"),
"type": left.get("type"),
"name": left.get("names", [None])[0],
"position": float(j.get("position", 0.0)),
"velocity": float(j.get("velocity", 0.0)),
"effort": float(j.get("effort", 0.0)),
"err_code": int(j.get("err_code", 0)),
}
return feedback["position"], feedback

def build_observation(robot, prompt):
joint_positions = serialize_joint_states(robot)
left_pos, feedback = read_left_gripper_feedback(robot)

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

247/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结
state = joint_positions + [float(left_pos)]
return {
"robot": "agibot_g2",
"prompt": prompt,
"state": state,
"state_dim": len(state),
"left_gripper_feedback": feedback,
"time_unix": time.time(),
}

def request_policy_server(observation):
msg = json.dumps(observation) + "\n"
with socket.create_connection((SERVER_HOST, SERVER_PORT), timeout=5.0)
sock.sendall(msg.encode("utf-8"))
buffer = b""
while b"\n" not in buffer:
data = sock.recv(4096)
if not data:
raise RuntimeError("policy server closed connection")
buffer += data
line = buffer.split(b"\n", 1)[0]
resp = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
if not resp.get("ok"):
raise RuntimeError(f"policy server error: {resp}")
return resp

def move_left_gripper(robot, target_position):
joint_states = agibot_gdk.JointStates()
joint_states.group = "left_tool"
joint_states.target_type = "omnipicker"
joint_state = agibot_gdk.JointState()
joint_state.position = float(target_position)
joint_states.states = [joint_state]
joint_states.nums = len(joint_states.states)
return robot.move_ee_pos(joint_states)

1cm，然后再回到原位。这样我们才开始真正进入“手臂控制”。

import json
import time
import math
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import numpy as np
import agibot_gdk

LEFT_EE_FRAME = "arm_l_end_link"
RIGHT_EE_FRAME = "arm_r_end_link"
GROUP_LEFT = 4
SERVO_RATE_HZ = 50.0
SERVO_LIFETIME = 0.02

def sleep_to_rate(next_t, period):
now = time.monotonic()
if next_t > now:
time.sleep(next_t - now)
return next_t + period

def pose_to_pos_quat(p):
pos = np.array([
float(p.position.x),
float(p.position.y),
float(p.position.z),
], dtype=np.float32)
quat = np.array([
float(p.orientation.x),
float(p.orientation.y),
float(p.orientation.z),
float(p.orientation.w),
], dtype=np.float32)
return pos, quat

def quat_normalize(q):
q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float32)
n = np.linalg.norm(q)
if n < 1e-8:
return np.array([0, 0, 0, 1], dtype=np.float32)
return q / n

def get_both_ee_poses(robot):
status = robot.get_motion_control_status()
if status.error_code != 0:
print("motion error_code:", status.error_code)
print("motion error_msg:", status.error_msg)
poses = {}
for name, pose in zip(status.frame_names, status.frame_poses):
if name == LEFT_EE_FRAME:
poses["left"] = pose_to_pos_quat(pose)
elif name == RIGHT_EE_FRAME:
poses["right"] = pose_to_pos_quat(pose)
if "left" not in poses:
raise RuntimeError(f"

找不到左臂末端 frame: {LEFT_EE_FRAME}")

if "right" not in poses:
raise RuntimeError(f"

找不到右臂末端 frame: {RIGHT_EE_FRAME}")

return poses["left"][0], poses["left"][1], poses["right"][0], poses["r

def fill_pose(dst_pose, pos, quat):
quat = quat_normalize(quat)
dst_pose.position.x = float(pos[0])
dst_pose.position.y = float(pos[1])
dst_pose.position.z = float(pos[2])
dst_pose.orientation.x = float(quat[0])
dst_pose.orientation.y = float(quat[1])
dst_pose.orientation.z = float(quat[2])
dst_pose.orientation.w = float(quat[3])

def send_dual_pose_keep_right(robot, left_pos, left_quat, right_pos, right_
end_pose = agibot_gdk.EndEffectorPose()
end_pose.life_time = SERVO_LIFETIME
end_pose.group = GROUP_LEFT
fill_pose(end_pose.left_end_effector_pose, left_pos, left_quat)
fill_pose(end_pose.right_end_effector_pose, right_pos, right_quat)
ret = robot.end_effector_pose_control(end_pose)
return ret

def servo_left_arm_to(robot, target_left_pos, target_left_quat, duration_s
"""

左臂末端插值移动到目标位姿。
右臂保持当前位姿不动。

"""
start_l_pos, start_l_quat, r_pos, r_quat = get_both_ee_poses(robot)
target_left_quat = quat_normalize(target_left_quat)
steps = max(1, int(duration_s * SERVO_RATE_HZ))
period = 1.0 / SERVO_RATE_HZ
next_t = time.monotonic()
last_ret = None
for i in range(steps):
t = (i + 1) / steps
cur_l_pos = start_l_pos * (1.0 - t) + target_left_pos * t
cur_l_quat = start_l_quat * (1.0 - t) + target_left_quat * t
cur_l_quat = quat_normalize(cur_l_quat)
last_ret = send_dual_pose_keep_right(
robot,
cur_l_pos,
cur_l_quat,
r_pos,
r_quat,
)
next_t = sleep_to_rate(next_t, period)
return last_ret

def hold_current_left_pose(robot, hold_s):
l_pos, l_quat, r_pos, r_quat = get_both_ee_poses(robot)
steps = max(1, int(hold_s * SERVO_RATE_HZ))
period = 1.0 / SERVO_RATE_HZ
next_t = time.monotonic()
for _ in range(steps):
send_dual_pose_keep_right(robot, l_pos, l_quat, r_pos, r_quat)
next_t = sleep_to_rate(next_t, period)

def main():

def sleep_to_rate(next_t, period):
now = time.monotonic()
if next_t > now:
time.sleep(next_t - now)
return next_t + period

def servo_left_to(robot, target_left_pos, target_left_quat, duration_s):
start_l_pos, start_l_quat, r_pos, r_quat = get_both_ee_poses(robot)
steps = max(1, int(duration_s * SERVO_RATE_HZ))
period = 1.0 / SERVO_RATE_HZ
next_t = time.monotonic()
last_ret = None
for i in range(steps):
alpha = (i + 1) / steps
cur_l_pos = start_l_pos * (1.0 - alpha) + target_left_pos * alpha
cur_l_quat = start_l_quat * (1.0 - alpha) + target_left_quat * alp
cur_l_quat = quat_normalize(cur_l_quat)
last_ret = send_left_pose_keep_right(
robot,
cur_l_pos,
cur_l_quat,
r_pos,
r_quat,
)
next_t = sleep_to_rate(next_t, period)
return last_ret

class G2ActionSafetyAdapterV2:
"""
G2 v0.2 action safety adapter.
action:
[dx, dy, dz, droll, dpitch, dyaw, gripper]

self.dy_max = 0.03

def sanitize_single(self, action):
action = self._check_action(action)
safe = np.zeros((7,), dtype=np.float32)

safe[1] = np.clip(action[1], self.dy_min, self.dy_max)
safe[2] = np.clip(action[2], self.dz_min, self.dz_max)

if not np.all(np.isfinite(actions)):

def sleep_to_rate(next_t, period):
now = time.monotonic()
if next_t > now:
time.sleep(next_t - now)
return next_t + period

def servo_left_to(robot, target_left_pos, target_left_quat, duration_s):
start_l_pos, start_l_quat, r_pos, r_quat = get_both_ee_poses(robot)
steps = max(1, int(duration_s * SERVO_RATE_HZ))
period = 1.0 / SERVO_RATE_HZ
next_t = time.monotonic()

robot,
cur_l_pos,
cur_l_quat,
r_pos,
r_quat,
)
next_t = sleep_to_rate(next_t, period)
return last_ret

def move_left_gripper(robot, target_position):

joint_states.group = "left_tool"
joint_states.target_type = "omnipicker"
joint_state = agibot_gdk.JointState()
joint_state.position = float(target_position)
joint_states.states = [joint_state]
joint_states.nums = len(joint_states.states)
return robot.move_ee_pos(joint_states)

2.

夹爪在 v2 runtime 里没有跟随目标

问题在这里：

STEP 1 gripper_target: -0.05
gripper_feedback position: -0.0
STEP 2 gripper_target: -0.10
gripper_feedback position: -0.0
STEP 3 gripper_target: -0.15
gripper_feedback position: -0.0

也就是说， ret_gripper: 0 虽然返回成功，但夹爪反馈一直是 -0.0 ，没有像之前单独
测试夹爪时那样变到 -0.04 / -0.09 / -0.14 。
这个问题大概率是因为当前 v2 脚本里执行顺序是：
先发 gripper 命令
马上进入 1.5 秒左臂 end_effector_pose_control 伺服
再读夹爪反馈

左臂末端伺服可能覆盖了末端执行器控制节奏，或者夹爪命令发出去后没有足够独立执行时
间。解决方式：改成先移动手臂，再执行夹爪，再等待 0.5 秒读反馈。
https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import sys
import json

ROOT = Path("~/g2_openpi").expanduser()

303/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

sys.path.append(str(ROOT / "g2_runtime"))
from g2_action_safety_adapter_v2 import G2ActionSafetyAdapterV2

SERVER_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
SERVER_PORT = 8766
LEFT_EE_FRAME = "arm_l_end_link"
RIGHT_EE_FRAME = "arm_r_end_link"
GROUP_LEFT = 4
SERVO_RATE_HZ = 50.0
SERVO_LIFETIME = 0.02

def quat_normalize(q):
q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float32)
n = np.linalg.norm(q)
if n < 1e-8:
return np.array([0, 0, 0, 1], dtype=np.float32)
return q / n

def pose_to_pos_quat(p):
pos = np.array([
float(p.position.x),
float(p.position.y),
float(p.position.z),
], dtype=np.float32)
quat = np.array([
float(p.orientation.x),
float(p.orientation.y),
float(p.orientation.z),
float(p.orientation.w),
], dtype=np.float32)
return pos, quat_normalize(quat)

def get_both_ee_poses(robot):
status = robot.get_motion_control_status()
poses = {}
for name, pose in zip(status.frame_names, status.frame_poses):
if name == LEFT_EE_FRAME:
poses["left"] = pose_to_pos_quat(pose)

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

def read_left_gripper_feedback(robot):

"position": None,
"err_code": None,
"name": None,
}

"position": float(j.get("position", 0.0)),
"velocity": float(j.get("velocity", 0.0)),
"effort": float(j.get("effort", 0.0)),
"err_code": int(j.get("err_code", 0)),
"name": left.get("names", [None])[0],
}
return feedback["position"], feedback

def build_observation(robot, prompt):
joint_positions = serialize_joint_states(robot)
left_gripper_pos, gripper_feedback = read_left_gripper_feedback(robot)
left_pos, left_quat, right_pos, right_quat = get_both_ee_poses(robot)

return {
"robot": "agibot_g2",
"prompt": prompt,
"state": state,
"state_dim": len(state),
"left_ee_pos": left_pos.tolist(),
"left_ee_quat": left_quat.tolist(),
"left_gripper_feedback": gripper_feedback,
"images": {
"base_0_rgb": "not_sent_in_v2_socket_test",
"left_wrist_0_rgb": "not_sent_in_v2_socket_test",
"right_wrist_0_rgb": "not_sent_in_v2_socket_test",
},
"time_unix": time.time(),
}

def request_policy_server(observation):
msg = json.dumps(observation) + "\n"
with socket.create_connection((SERVER_HOST, SERVER_PORT), timeout=5.0)
sock.sendall(msg.encode("utf-8"))
buffer = b""
while b"\n" not in buffer:
data = sock.recv(4096)
if not data:
raise RuntimeError("policy server closed connection")
buffer += data
line = buffer.split(b"\n", 1)[0]
resp = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
if not resp.get("ok"):
raise RuntimeError(f"policy server error: {resp}")
return resp

def sleep_to_rate(next_t, period):
now = time.monotonic()
if next_t > now:
time.sleep(next_t - now)
return next_t + period

def servo_left_to(robot, target_left_pos, target_left_quat, duration_s):
start_l_pos, start_l_quat, r_pos, r_quat = get_both_ee_poses(robot)
steps = max(1, int(duration_s * SERVO_RATE_HZ))
period = 1.0 / SERVO_RATE_HZ
next_t = time.monotonic()
last_ret = None
for i in range(steps):
alpha = (i + 1) / steps
cur_l_pos = start_l_pos * (1.0 - alpha) + target_left_pos * alpha
cur_l_quat = start_l_quat * (1.0 - alpha) + target_left_quat * alp
cur_l_quat = quat_normalize(cur_l_quat)
last_ret = send_left_pose_keep_right(
robot,
cur_l_pos,
cur_l_quat,

def move_left_gripper(robot, target_position):
joint_states = agibot_gdk.JointStates()
joint_states.group = "left_tool"
joint_states.target_type = "omnipicker"
joint_state = agibot_gdk.JointState()
joint_state.position = float(target_position)
joint_states.states = [joint_state]
joint_states.nums = len(joint_states.states)
return robot.move_ee_pos(joint_states)

Thought for 11s

这个报错很明确，不是代码问题，是 8766 端口已经被占用了：
OSError: [Errno 98] Address already in use

也就是说， g2_mock_policy_server_v2.py 已经有一个实例在跑，或者之前启动的进程
没有退出，所以新的 server 绑定不了 8766 。
你现在有两种处理方式。
Pasted text

方式 A：如果另一个终端里 server 还在跑

不要重复启动 server。直接打开另一个终端跑 client：
conda deactivate 2>/dev/null || true
cd ~/g2_openpi
source ~/app/env.sh
Bash g2_runtime/g2_policy_client_arm_gripper_runtime_v2.py
python3

如果 client 能连上，就说明 server 已经在后台或另一个终端正常监听。

方式 B：找出占用 8766 的进程并杀掉
执行：

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

def quat_normalize(q):
q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float32)
n = np.linalg.norm(q)
if n < 1e-8:
return np.array([0, 0, 0, 1], dtype=np.float32)
return q / n

try:

with conn:
buffer = b""
while True:
data = conn.recv(65536)
if not data:
break
buffer += data
while b"\n" in buffer:

continue

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import sys
import json
import time
import socket
import base64
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
import cv2
import numpy as np

G2 GDK研究总结

ROOT = Path("~/g2_openpi").expanduser()
sys.path.append(str(ROOT / "g2_runtime"))
from g2_action_safety_adapter_v2 import G2ActionSafetyAdapterV2

SERVER_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
SERVER_PORT = 8878
LEFT_EE_FRAME = "arm_l_end_link"
RIGHT_EE_FRAME = "arm_r_end_link"
GROUP_LEFT = 4
SERVO_RATE_HZ = 50.0
SERVO_LIFETIME = 0.02

def image_data_to_bytes(data):
if hasattr(data, "tobytes"):
return data.tobytes()
return bytes(data)

def decode_gdk_image(image):
if image is None or not hasattr(image, "data") or image.data is None:
return None
raw = image_data_to_bytes(image.data)
try:
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.JPEG:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, np.uint8)
return cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.PNG:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, np.uint8)
return cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
if image.encoding == agibot_gdk.Encoding.UNCOMPRESSED:
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.RGB:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
rgb = arr.reshape((image.height, image.width, 3))
return cv2.cvtColor(rgb, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.BGR:
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
return arr.reshape((image.height, image.width, 3))
if image.color_format == agibot_gdk.ColorFormat.GRAY8:

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

334/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
gray = arr.reshape((image.height, image.width))
return cv2.cvtColor(gray, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)
return None
except Exception as e:
print("[WARN] decode image failed:", e)
return None

def encode_image_for_socket(bgr, quality=80):
resized = cv2.resize(bgr, (224, 224), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
ok, buf = cv2.imencode(
".jpg",
resized,
[int(cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY), int(quality)],
)
if not ok:
raise RuntimeError("cv2.imencode jpg failed")
b64 = base64.b64encode(buf.tobytes()).decode("utf-8")
return {
"encoding": "jpeg_base64",
"width": 224,
"height": 224,
"channels": 3,
"data_b64": b64,
}

def capture_images(camera):
camera_specs = [
(agibot_gdk.CameraType.kHeadColor, "base_0_rgb"),
(agibot_gdk.CameraType.kHandLeftColor, "left_wrist_0_rgb"),
(agibot_gdk.CameraType.kHandRightColor, "right_wrist_0_rgb"),
]
result = {}
summary = {}
for camera_type, key in camera_specs:
image = camera.get_latest_image(camera_type, 1000.0)
bgr = decode_gdk_image(image)
if bgr is None:

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

G2 GDK研究总结
raise RuntimeError(f"failed to capture/decode camera: {key}")
result[key] = encode_image_for_socket(bgr, quality=80)
summary[key] = {
"raw_width": int(image.width),
"raw_height": int(image.height),
"encoded_width": 224,
"encoded_height": 224,
"mean": float(bgr.mean()),
}
return result, summary

def quat_normalize(q):
q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float32)
n = np.linalg.norm(q)
if n < 1e-8:
return np.array([0, 0, 0, 1], dtype=np.float32)
return q / n

def pose_to_pos_quat(p):
pos = np.array([
float(p.position.x),
float(p.position.y),
float(p.position.z),
], dtype=np.float32)
quat = np.array([
float(p.orientation.x),
float(p.orientation.y),
float(p.orientation.z),
float(p.orientation.w),
], dtype=np.float32)
return pos, quat_normalize(quat)

def get_both_ee_poses(robot):
status = robot.get_motion_control_status()
poses = {}
for name, pose in zip(status.frame_names, status.frame_poses):
if name == LEFT_EE_FRAME:
poses["left"] = pose_to_pos_quat(pose)
elif name == RIGHT_EE_FRAME:
poses["right"] = pose_to_pos_quat(pose)

def read_left_gripper_feedback(robot):
end_state = robot.get_end_state()
left = end_state.get("left_end_state", {})
joints = left.get("end_states", [])
if not joints:
return 0.0, {
"position": None,
"err_code": None,
"name": None,
}
j = joints[0]
feedback = {
"position": float(j.get("position", 0.0)),
"velocity": float(j.get("velocity", 0.0)),
"effort": float(j.get("effort", 0.0)),
"err_code": int(j.get("err_code", 0)),
"name": left.get("names", [None])[0],
}
return feedback["position"], feedback

def request_policy_server(observation):
msg = json.dumps(observation) + "\n"
with socket.create_connection((SERVER_HOST, SERVER_PORT), timeout=10.0
sock.sendall(msg.encode("utf-8"))
buffer = b""
while b"\n" not in buffer:
data = sock.recv(65536)
if not data:
raise RuntimeError("policy server closed connection")
buffer += data
line = buffer.split(b"\n", 1)[0]
resp = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
if not resp.get("ok"):
raise RuntimeError(f"policy server error: {resp}")
return resp

dst_pose.position.x = float(pos[0])
dst_pose.position.y = float(pos[1])

def sleep_to_rate(next_t, period):
now = time.monotonic()
if next_t > now:
time.sleep(next_t - now)
return next_t + period

def servo_left_to(robot, target_left_pos, target_left_quat, duration_s):
start_l_pos, start_l_quat, r_pos, r_quat = get_both_ee_poses(robot)
steps = max(1, int(duration_s * SERVO_RATE_HZ))
period = 1.0 / SERVO_RATE_HZ
next_t = time.monotonic()
last_ret = None
for i in range(steps):
alpha = (i + 1) / steps
cur_l_pos = start_l_pos * (1.0 - alpha) + target_left_pos * alpha
cur_l_quat = start_l_quat * (1.0 - alpha) + target_left_quat * alp
cur_l_quat = quat_normalize(cur_l_quat)
last_ret = send_left_pose_keep_right(
robot,
cur_l_pos,
cur_l_quat,
r_pos,
r_quat,
)

def move_left_gripper(robot, target_position):

return robot.move_ee_pos(joint_states)

# G2 ↔ pi0.5 Policy Server Contract v0.3
## Runtime Goal
G2 client sends one VLA observation to policy server.
Policy server returns an action chunk.
G2 client applies safety adapter and executes:
- left arm dx / dy / dz
- left gripper target
Rotation is locked:
- droll = 0
- dpitch = 0
- dyaw = 0

# G2 ↔ pi0.5 Policy Server Contract v0.3
## 1. Runtime Goal
G2 client sends one VLA observation to policy server.
Policy server returns an action chunk.
G2 client applies safety adapter and executes:
- left arm dx / dy / dz
- left gripper target
Rotation is locked for now:
- droll = 0
- dpitch = 0
- dyaw = 0

- robot: agibot_g2

- state: 23 floats
- state_dim: 23
- left_ee_pos: [x, y, z]
- left_ee_quat: [qx, qy, qz, qw]

- images

356/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

Each image is:
- encoding: jpeg_base64
- width: 224
- height: 224
- channels: 3
- data_b64: base64 string
--## 3. Response Format
One response is one JSON line.
Main fields:
- ok: true
- server: server name
- actions: action chunk
- action_shape: [T, 7]
Each action is:
[dx, dy, dz, droll, dpitch, dyaw, gripper]
--## 4. Action Meaning
action[0] = dx, meters
action[1] = dy, meters
action[2] = dz, meters
action[3] = droll, currently locked to 0
action[4] = dpitch, currently locked to 0
action[5] = dyaw, currently locked to 0
action[6] = left gripper target position
--## 5. Current Safety Limits
dx: [-0.03, 0.03] m per step
dy: [-0.03, 0.03] m per step
dz: [-0.04, 0.04] m per step
droll / dpitch / dyaw: forced to 0
gripper: [-0.15, 0.0]

注意最后一行 EOF 必须是单独一行，前后不要加空格。

你刚才为什么出现 } 那些奇怪内容？

因为你在 cat <<'MD' 还没结束的时候继续粘贴了别的内容。Shell 不会把那些当命令执
行，而是全部当成文件内容写进去，所以看起来就乱了。

PORT = 8890

def decode_image_b64(image_obj):
raw = base64.b64decode(image_obj["data_b64"].encode("utf-8"))
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
img = cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
if img is None:
raise RuntimeError("image decode failed")
if img.shape[:2] != (224, 224):
raise RuntimeError(f"image shape should be 224x224, got {img.shape
return img

raise RuntimeError(f"state shape should be (23,), got {state.shape
image_arrays = {
"base_0_rgb": decode_image_b64(images["base_0_rgb"]),
"left_wrist_0_rgb": decode_image_b64(images["left_wrist_0_rgb"]),
"right_wrist_0_rgb": decode_image_b64(images["right_wrist_0_rgb"])
}
return {
"prompt": prompt,
"state": state,
"images": image_arrays,

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结
while True:
conn, addr = server.accept()
print("\nclient connected:", addr)
with conn:
buffer = b""
while True:
data = conn.recv(65536)
if not data:
break
buffer += data
while b"\n" in buffer:
line, buffer = buffer.split(b"\n", 1)
if not line.strip():
continue
try:
req = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
resp = handle_request(req)
except Exception as e:
resp = {
"ok": False,
"error": repr(e),
}
conn.sendall((json.dumps(resp) + "\n").encode("utf
print("client disconnected:", addr)
finally:
server.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
main()

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

这是同一个问题：8890 端口已经有进程在监听了，所以你新启动
g2_pi05_policy_server_stub.py 时绑定失败：
OSError: [Errno 98] Address already in use

你这里的关键输出是：
LISTEN 0 5 0.0.0.0:8890

说明 8890 上已经有 server 在跑。现在有两种走法。

方案 A：直接跑 client

如果 8890 上跑的正好就是刚才的 stub server，那你不用再启动 server，直接开另一个终
端跑：
conda deactivate 2>/dev/null || true
cd ~/g2_openpi
source ~/app/env.sh
Bash g2_runtime/g2_policy_client_arm_gripper_runtime_v3_image.py
python3

如果看到：
server: g2_pi05_policy_server_stub
action_shape: [6, 7]

就说明没问题。

方案 B：查出并杀掉占用 8890 的进程
你现在 ss 没显示 PID，所以用下面这个：
Bash
sudo
ss -ltnp | grep ':8890'

或者：
https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

369/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

Bash
sudo
lsof -iTCP:8890 -sTCP:LISTEN -P -n

或者：
Bash
sudo
fuser -v 8890/tcp

找到 PID 后杀掉，比如 PID 是 123456 ：
Bash
kill
123456

如果杀不掉：
Bash
kill
-9 123456

然后确认端口释放：
Bash | grep ':8890' || echo "8890 is free"
ss -ltnp

再重新启动 server：
cd ~/g2_openpi
Bash g2_runtime/g2_pi05_policy_server_stub.py
python3

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import os
import sys

import glob
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

def run(cmd):
try:
out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, shell=True, stderr=subprocess.S
return out.strip()
except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
return e.output.strip()

def find_paths():
candidates = []
search_roots = [
Path("~/g2_openpi").expanduser(),
Path("~").expanduser(),
Path("~/openpi").expanduser(),
Path("~/OpenPI").expanduser(),
Path("~/pi0").expanduser(),
Path("~/pi05").expanduser(),
Path("~/pi0.5").expanduser(),
]
keywords = [
"openpi",

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结
"pi0",
"pi05",
"pi0_5",
"checkpoint",
"checkpoints",
"params",
"weights",
]
for root in search_roots:
if not root.exists():
continue
for keyword in keywords:
for p in root.rglob(f"*{keyword}*"):
try:
if len(str(p)) < 300:
candidates.append(str(p))
except Exception:
pass
return sorted(set(candidates))

def main():
print("=== G2 OpenPI / pi0.5 Environment Probe ===")
info = {
"python": sys.executable,
"python_version": sys.version,
"cwd": os.getcwd(),
"conda_prefix": os.environ.get("CONDA_PREFIX"),
"pythonpath": os.environ.get("PYTHONPATH"),
"path_candidates": find_paths()[:200],
"commands": {
"which_python": run("which python3 || which python"),
"conda_envs": run("conda info --envs 2>/dev/null || true"),
"pip_openpi": run("python3 -m pip show openpi 2>/dev/null || t
"pip_torch": run("python3 -m pip show torch 2>/dev/null || tru
"pip_jax": run("python3 -m pip show jax 2>/dev/null || true"),
"pip_flax": run("python3 -m pip show flax 2>/dev/null || true"
"pip_transformers": run("python3 -m pip show transformers 2>/d
"nvidia_smi": run("nvidia-smi 2>/dev/null || true"),
}
}
out_path = Path("~/g2_openpi/openpi_env_probe.json").expanduser()
with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
json.dump(info, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

Jetson Thor 上也有专门的 OpenPI π0.5 部署路线，核心是 JAX checkpoint → PyTorch →
ONNX → TensorRT 的推理加速链路。
GitHub

Jetson AI Lab

因为 pi05_droid 的配置里已经显示：

def make_image(value=127):
return np.full((224, 224, 3), value, dtype=np.uint8)

"right_wrist_0_rgb": make_image(140),
},

"left_wrist_0_rgb": True,
"right_wrist_0_rgb": True,
},

},
})

"wrist_image_left": make_image(140),

import time
import traceback
import numpy as np
from openpi.training import config as _config
from openpi.policies import policy_config

def make_image(value):
return np.full((224, 224, 3), value, dtype=np.uint8)

def make_droid_obs():
return {
"observation/exterior_image_1_left": make_image(120),
"observation/wrist_image_left": make_image(135),
# pi05_droid expects DROID-style 7-dim joint position.
# For now this is dummy state, not G2 state.
"observation/joint_position": np.zeros((7,), dtype=np.float32),
# Must be shape (1,), not scalar.
"observation/gripper_position": np.zeros((1,), dtype=np.float32),
"prompt": "pick up the object",
}

def summarize_actions(actions):
actions = np.asarray(actions)
print("actions shape:", actions.shape)
print("actions dtype:", actions.dtype)
print("actions min:", float(actions.min()))
print("actions max:", float(actions.max()))
print("actions mean:", float(actions.mean()))
print("first action:", actions[0])
print("last action:", actions[-1])
if actions.ndim == 2:
print("\nper-dim stats:")
for i in range(actions.shape[1]):
col = actions[:, i]
print(
f"dim {i:02d}: "
f"min={float(col.min()): .6f}, "
f"max={float(col.max()): .6f}, "
f"mean={float(col.mean()): .6f}"
)

428/929

2026/4/27 17:35

G2 GDK研究总结

[OUTPUT]
keys: ['actions']
actions shape: (15, 8)

为什么是 (15, 8) ？
因为 pi05_droid 配置里 action_horizon=15 ，内部模型是 action_dim=32 ，但
DROID policy 的输出 transform 会只返回前 8 维。DROID 示例里 action 是 7维 joint
velocity + 1维 gripper position 。
droid_policy

from openpi.policies import policy_config
from openpi.models import model as _model

def make_image(value):
return np.full((224, 224, 3), value, dtype=np.uint8)

def make_droid_obs():
return {
"observation/exterior_image_1_left": make_image(120),
"observation/wrist_image_left": make_image(135),
"observation/joint_position": np.zeros((7,), dtype=np.float32),
"observation/gripper_position": np.zeros((1,), dtype=np.float32),
"prompt": "pick up the object",
}

def summarize_actions(actions):
actions = np.asarray(actions)
print("actions shape:", actions.shape)
print("actions dtype:", actions.dtype)
print("actions min:", float(actions.min()))
print("actions max:", float(actions.max()))
print("actions mean:", float(actions.mean()))
print("first action:", actions[0])
print("last action:", actions[-1])
if actions.ndim == 2:
print("\nper-dim stats:")
for i in range(actions.shape[1]):
col = actions[:, i]
print(
f"dim {i:02d}: "
f"min={float(col.min()): .6f}, "
f"max={float(col.max()): .6f}, "
f"mean={float(col.mean()): .6f}"
)

def force_restore_params_fp32():
original_restore_params = _model.restore_params
def restore_params_fp32(params_path, dtype=None):
print("[PATCH] forcing restore_params dtype = jnp.float32")
return original_restore_params(params_path, dtype=jnp.float32)
_model.restore_params = restore_params_fp32

Thought for 14s

这个截图里的内容不是报错，是 JAX/XLA 在 Thor GPU 上第一次编译 pi0.5 模型时反复打印
的 warning：
Unknown compute capability 11.0. Defaulting to telling LLVM that we'r

现在看到的现象说明：

✅ 程序还在运行
✅ 已经进入 GPU 编译阶段
✅ 不是 ptxas too old 那个致命错误
✅ 也不是 BF16/F16 那个 LLVM ERROR
你现在先不要中断。第一次 policy.infer() 会触发大量 JIT 编译，pi05_droid 模型比较
大，Thor 上第一次编译会刷很多这种 warning。

你现在开另一个终端看它有没有在工作
不要动当前运行的终端。新开一个终端执行：
https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

译/推理。

判断标准

继续观察当前终端，最终会出现三种情况之一。

情况 1：成功

你会看到类似：
[OUTPUT]
keys: ['actions']

pi05_droid
DROID action

def decode_image_b64_to_rgb(image_obj):
raw = base64.b64decode(image_obj["data_b64"].encode("utf-8"))
arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8)
bgr = cv2.imdecode(arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)

return rgb.astype(np.uint8)

def g2_gripper_to_droid_gripper(g2_gripper_pos):
"""
G2
[-0.15, 0.0]
DROID
0~1
"""
try:
g = float(g2_gripper_pos)
except Exception:
g = 0.0

return np.asarray([close_ratio], dtype=np.float32)

def build_droid_obs_from_g2_request(req):
images = req.get("images", {})
exterior_rgb = decode_image_b64_to_rgb(images["base_0_rgb"])
wrist_rgb = decode_image_b64_to_rgb(images["left_wrist_0_rgb"])
left_ee_pos = req.get("left_ee_pos", [0.0, 0.0, 0.0])
left_ee_quat = req.get("left_ee_quat", [0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0])
joint_position = np.asarray(
list(left_ee_pos[:3]) + list(left_ee_quat[:4]),
dtype=np.float32,
)
if joint_position.shape != (7,):
joint_position = np.zeros((7,), dtype=np.float32)
feedback = req.get("left_gripper_feedback", {})
gripper_pos = feedback.get("position", None)
if gripper_pos is None:
state = req.get("state", [])
gripper_pos = state[-1] if state else 0.0
gripper_position = g2_gripper_to_droid_gripper(gripper_pos)

def handle_request(req, policy, projector):

print("droid gripper_position:", obs_summary["gripper_position"])

out = policy.infer(droid_obs)
infer_time = time.time() - t0
pi_actions = np.asarray(out["actions"], dtype=np.float32)
g2_actions = projector.project_chunk(pi_actions)
if RETURN_HORIZON > 0:
g2_actions_to_return = g2_actions[:RETURN_HORIZON]
else:
g2_actions_to_return = g2_actions
print("[PI05]")
print("infer_time_s:", infer_time)
print("raw pi actions:", pi_actions.shape)
print("projected g2 actions:", g2_actions_to_return.shape)
return {
"ok": True,
"server": "g2_pi05_droid_policy_server_cpu_projected",
"created_at": time.time(),
"infer_time_s": infer_time,
"return_horizon": int(g2_actions_to_return.shape[0]),
"droid_obs_summary": obs_summary,
"raw_pi05_summary": summarize_actions(pi_actions),
"projected_g2_summary": summarize_actions(g2_actions_to_return),
"actions": g2_actions_to_return.tolist(),

推理每次约

policy = load_policy()
projector = G2Pi05DroidActionProjector()
server = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
server.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
server.bind((HOST, PORT))
server.listen(1)
try:
while True:
conn, addr = server.accept()
print("\nclient connected:", addr)
with conn:
buffer = b""
while True:
data = conn.recv(65536)
if not data:
break
buffer += data
while b"\n" in buffer:
line, buffer = buffer.split(b"\n", 1)
if not line.strip():
continue
try:
req = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
resp = handle_request(req, policy, projector)
except Exception as e:
traceback.print_exc()
resp = {
"ok": False,
"error": repr(e),
}

def request_policy_server(observation):
msg = json.dumps(observation) + "\n"
with socket.create_connection((SERVER_HOST, SERVER_PORT), timeout=10.0
sock.settimeout(SOCKET_TIMEOUT)
sock.sendall(msg.encode("utf-8"))
buffer = b""
while b"\n" not in buffer:
data = sock.recv(65536)
if not data:
raise RuntimeError("policy server closed connection")
buffer += data
line = buffer.split(b"\n", 1)[0]
resp = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
if not resp.get("ok"):
raise RuntimeError(f"policy server error: {resp}")
return resp

def summarize_actions(actions):
arr = np.asarray(actions, dtype=np.float32)
print("actions shape:", arr.shape)
print("actions dtype:", arr.dtype)
print("actions min:", float(arr.min()))
print("actions max:", float(arr.max()))
print("actions mean:", float(arr.mean()))
print("first:", arr[0])
print("last:", arr[-1])
print("per-dim stats:")
for i in range(arr.shape[1]):
c = arr[:, i]
print(
f" dim {i:02d}: "
f"min={float(c.min()): .6f}, "
f"max={float(c.max()): .6f}, "
f"mean={float(c.mean()): .6f}"
)

G2 GDK研究总结

SERVER_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
SERVER_PORT = 19010
SOCKET_TIMEOUT = 300.0

def request_policy_server(observation):
msg = json.dumps(observation) + "\n"
with socket.create_connection((SERVER_HOST, SERVER_PORT), timeout=10.0
sock.settimeout(SOCKET_TIMEOUT)
sock.sendall(msg.encode("utf-8"))
buffer = b""
while b"\n" not in buffer:
data = sock.recv(65536)
if not data:
raise RuntimeError("policy server closed connection")
buffer += data
line = buffer.split(b"\n", 1)[0]
resp = json.loads(line.decode("utf-8"))
if not resp.get("ok"):
raise RuntimeError(f"policy server error: {resp}")
return resp

def extra_guard_actions(actions, max_steps, max_xy, max_down_z, max_up_z,
"""

二次硬保护。
输入是 server 已经投影过的 G2 action: [T, 7]
输出仍是 G2 action: [T, 7]

"""
arr = np.asarray(actions, dtype=np.float32)
if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.shape[1] != 7:
raise RuntimeError(f"expected actions [T, 7], got {arr.shape}")
arr = arr[:max_steps].copy()
arr[:, 0] = np.clip(arr[:, 0], -max_xy, max_xy)
arr[:, 1] = np.clip(arr[:, 1], -max_xy, max_xy)
arr[:, 2] = np.clip(arr[:, 2], -max_down_z, max_up_z)

姿态强制锁死

#
arr[:, 3:6] = 0.0

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

# gripper_cap=0.03
-0.03
arr[:, 6] = np.clip(arr[:, 6], -abs(gripper_cap), 0.0)
return arr

def summarize_actions(name, actions):
arr = np.asarray(actions, dtype=np.float32)
print(f"\n[{name}]")
print("shape:", arr.shape)
print("min:", float(arr.min()))
print("max:", float(arr.max()))
print("mean:", float(arr.mean()))
print("first:", arr[0])
print("last:", arr[-1])
for i in range(arr.shape[1]):
c = arr[:, i]
print(
f" dim {i:02d}: "
f"min={float(c.min()): .6f}, "
f"max={float(c.max()): .6f}, "
f"mean={float(c.mean()): .6f}"
)

def execute_guarded_actions(robot, actions, arm_duration, execute_gripper)

print("target_pos:", target_l_pos.tolist())
print("gripper_target:", gripper_target)
ret_arm = servo_left_to(
robot,
target_l_pos,
cur_l_quat,
duration_s=arm_duration,
)

现在不要急着放大动作

你现在已经让真机执行过两步 pi05 投影动作，下一步应该先做两个“保险动作”：
1.
2.

写一个 reset 脚本：打开夹爪，并把左臂回到执行前位置
写一个日志检查脚本：自动判断 ret_arm、ret_gripper、pos_error、err_code 是

先把这两个补上，后面再逐步增加 step 数。

第 1 步：创建左臂回退 + 夹爪打开脚本

这个脚本会读取最近一次 pi05_guarded_execute_*.json ，从里面反推执行前的左臂起
点，然后把左臂慢速返回，同时把夹爪打开到 0.0 。

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import sys
import json
import glob
import time
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import agibot_gdk

ROOT = Path("~/g2_openpi").expanduser()

def find_latest_exec_log():
paths = sorted(glob.glob(str(ROOT / "runtime_logs" / "pi05_guarded_exe
paths = list(reversed(paths))
for p in paths:

我这个G2不是nvdia thor么，怎么是cpu，不是GPU
Thought for 25s

你的 G2 确实是 NVIDIA Thor，GPU 也确实存在。现在显示成 CPU，不是因为机器不是
GPU，而是因为我们主动把 pi0.5 推理切到了 CPU。
原因很简单：之前我们试过 GPU 路线，JAX 能识别 Thor GPU，简单 JIT 也能跑；但是
pi05_droid 真正推理时遇到了 Thor 上的编译兼容问题：
Unsupported conversion from bf16 to f16
LLVM ERROR: Unsupported rounding mode for conversion.

所以为了先把完整链路跑通，我让你用的是这个命令：
JAX_PLATFORM_NAME=cpu \
OPENPI_CONFIG=pi05_droid \

https://chatgpt.com/c/69eb2f6c-ca14-8398-9a55-0b6c26408401

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import traceback
import time
import jax
import jax.numpy as jnp

def run_cmd(cmd):
try:
out = subprocess.check_output(cmd, shell=True, stderr=subprocess.S
return out.strip()
except Exception as e:
return repr(e)

def run_case(name, fn):
print("\n" + "=" * 70)
print("CASE:", name)
t0 = time.time()
try:
y = fn()
if hasattr(y, "block_until_ready"):
y = y.block_until_ready()
print("
PASS")
print("time_s:", time.time() - t0)
print("result:", y)
return True
except Exception:
print("
FAIL")
print("time_s:", time.time() - t0)
traceback.print_exc()

通过：

2. no_autotune
fp32_add PASS
bf16_add PASS
f16_add PASS
bf16_to_f16 PASS
fp32_matmul PASS
bf16_matmul PASS
f16_matmul PASS

这说明 Thor GPU 不是不能用，BF16/F16 基础算子也不是完全不能用；真正触发崩溃的是
XLA 的 GPU autotune / profiler / redzone 相关路径。你把 XLA_FLAGS="-xla_gpu_autotune_level=0" 加上以后，基础诊断全部通过。

G2 GDK研究总结

Jax plugin configuration error:
jax_plugins.xla_cuda12.initialize()
ALREADY_EXISTS: PJRT_Api already exists for device type cuda
Loaded runtime CuDNN library: 9.10.1 but source was compiled with: 9.
AttributeError:
module 'jax.experimental.layout' has no attribute 'DeviceLocalLayout'

所以现在不是 Thor 不能跑 GPU，而是这个 .venv_gpu 同时混进了 CUDA12 和 CUDA13
两套 JAX/CUDA 组件，导致插件、cuDNN、Orbax/JAX API 全部互相打架。
JAX 官方 CUDA13 安装路径本身是支持 CUDA13 的，并要求 Linux CUDA13 驱动版本至少
580；你机器 nvidia-smi 显示 driver 580，所以硬件和驱动方向没错。现在要把 Python
环境清干净。

Thought for 22s

可以，这次其实已经推进到 checkpoint/Orbax 兼容层 了，不是 GPU/JAX 基础问题了。
你现在已经确认：

✅ openpi_client 已安装成功
✅ openpi_client.image_tools OK
✅ openpi OK
✅ jax 0.10.0 / jaxlib 0.10.0 OK
✅ Thor GPU 可见：devices: [CudaDevice(id=0)]
✅ backend: gpu
当前新错误是：
TypeError: 'StepMetadata' object is not subscriptable

出错位置：
/data/agi/openpi_gpu_exp/openpi/src/openpi/models/model.py
item = {"params": metadata["params"]}

这说明 OpenPI 当前源码期望旧版 Orbax 的 metadata 是 dict-like，但你 GPU 实验环境
里装了新版 orbax-checkpoint 0.11.36 ，它返回的是 StepMetadata 对象，所以不支持
metadata["params"] 。日志也显示你之前安装的是新版 flax 0.12.7 / orbaxcheckpoint 0.11.36 ，这和 OpenPI 原环境不一致。

Python {"params": {...
{"params":

Run

但 Orbax 需要的是：
真正参数...}}

Python {...
{"params":

Run

所以我们要把 restore_params 的 patch 改得更稳一点：如果新版 Orbax 的
metadata.item_metadata 已经包含顶层 "params" ，就只取里面那层，不要再包一层。

# Compatibility patch for newer Orbax StepMetadata.
# Old Orbax: metadata["params"] returns the parameter metadata sub

2026/4/27 17:35

# New Orbax: metadata.item_metadata may already be {"params": para
def _make_restore_item(metadata):
try:
return {"params": metadata["params"]}
except Exception:
pass
md = None
for attr in ("item_metadata", "metadata", "params"):
if hasattr(metadata, attr):
md = getattr(metadata, attr)
break
if md is None:
raise TypeError(
f"Unsupported Orbax metadata type: {type(metadata)}, a
)
# If md itself is the full restore tree {"params": ...}, unwra
try:
return {"params": md["params"]}
except Exception:
return {"params": md}
item = _make_restore_item(metadata)

'''
text = text[:start] + new_block + text[end:]
p.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")

repatched", p)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*import os
import time
import traceback
import dataclasses
import numpy as np
import jax.numpy as jnp
from openpi.training import config as _config
from openpi.policies import policy_config
from openpi.models import model as _model

def make_image(value):
return np.full((224, 224, 3), value, dtype=np.uint8)

def make_droid_obs(prompt="pick up the object"):
return {
"observation/exterior_image_1_left": make_image(120),
"observation/wrist_image_left": make_image(135),
"observation/joint_position": np.zeros((7,), dtype=np.float32),
"observation/gripper_position": np.zeros((1,), dtype=np.float32),
"prompt": prompt,
}

def force_restore_params_fp32():
original_restore_params = _model.restore_params
def restore_params_fp32(params_path, dtype=None):
print("[PATCH] forcing restore_params dtype = jnp.float32")
return original_restore_params(params_path, dtype=jnp.float32)
_model.restore_params = restore_params_fp32

def make_config_fp32(config_name):
cfg = _config.get_config(config_name)
print("[PATCH] original model dtype:", getattr(cfg.model, "dtype", Non
new_model = dataclasses.replace(cfg.model, dtype="float32")
new_cfg = dataclasses.replace(cfg, model=new_model)
print("[PATCH] patched model dtype:", getattr(new_cfg.model, "dtype",
